## RU

### Эксперт по красоте

В Кур-де-Фонтейне проходит какая-то акция. Здесь многолюдно и оживлённо.

#### Прогуляйтесь по Кур-де-Фонтейну

**Паймон:** Здесь так оживлённо. А что это за мероприятие?

**Паймон:** Хм... Презентация нового косметического продукта «Роса бабочки»? Похоже, реклама косметики.

**Паймон:** И Сиджвин тут?

**Паймон:** Привет, Сиджвин!

- **Путешественник:** Вот так совпадение.

- **Путешественник:** Давно не виделись.

**Сиджвин:** Привет, Путешественник и Паймон! Надеюсь, вы пребываете в добром здравии?

**Паймон:** Так и есть! А ты что здесь делаешь?

**Сиджвин:** По настоянию герцога я регулярно посещаю Кур-де-Фонтейн для отдыха.

**Сиджвин:** В этот раз, по чистой случайности, я оказалась на рекламном мероприятии нового продукта по уходу за кожей. Иногда я слежу за тенденциями в этой индустрии, чтобы писать статьи для журнала.

**Паймон:** Ты пишешь для журнала? Для какого?

**Сиджвин:** Журнал «Красота сегодня» под редакцией Ассоциации красоты Фонтейна. Я опубликовала много статей по уходу за кожей. Мой псевдоним - Ромарин.

**Паймон:** Что? Сиджвин - Ромарин, автор статей в журнале «Красота сегодня»? Если Паймон не ошибается, ты значимая фигура Ассоциации красоты Фонтейна...

Паймон, с каких это пор ты читаешь журналы красоты?

**Паймон:** Паймон любит красоту, что в этом удивительного? Все любят красоту.

**Сиджвин:** Мне нравится эта работа. Сотрудники магазинов косметики всегда очень вежливы со мной. Мне всего лишь нужно улыбаться, и они сами приложат все усилия, чтобы продемонстрировать наилучшее качество обслуживания.

**Паймон:** Наверняка владельцы не хотят, чтобы Ромарин оставила негативные отзывы.

- **Путешественник:** Почему ты так интересуешься красотой, Сиджвин?

- **Путешественник:** Как ты стала одним из главных лиц в Ассоциации красоты?

**Паймон:** Это значит, что Сиджвин тоже любит красоту. Но иметь вес в Ассоциации красоты - это нечто потрясающее...

**Сиджвин:** Если быть точной, я изучаю эту область, потому что дорожу своей внешностью. Это не имеет ничего общего с красотой или её отсутствием.

**Паймон:** Ха... Слова истинного мастера. Минуточку, ты хочешь сказать, что естественная красота - высочайшая степень красоты?

**Сиджвин:** Не совсем... По сути, эстетические представления мелюзин сильно отличаются от людских, поэтому мы не оцениваем людей по их внешности.

**Сиджвин:** Если бы я описывала наш подход, я бы сказала, что он основан на миловидности.

**Паймон:** Вот как?

**Сиджвин:** Да, я ценю свою внешность, и благодаря этому у меня накопился большой опыт по уходу за собой.

**Сиджвин:** То, что этот опыт снискал огромную популярность среди любителей косметики, - случайность.

**Паймон:** Вот теперь Паймон поняла. Кстати, о твоей внешности... Почему ты выглядишь как человек? У тебя... смешанная кровь?

**Паймон:** Ой, прости! Это было бестактно...

**Сиджвин:** Не извиняйся, всё в порядке. Я приняла эту форму по собственной воле очень-очень давно.

**Сиджвин:** Я могу рассказать эту историю, если вам интересно.

**Паймон:** Постой, так ты сама решила стать такой? О, Паймон теперь очень любопытно!

**Сиджвин:** Давным-давно...

**Сиджвин:** Так и получилось, что с тех пор я живу в человеческом облике. Большинство людей, которые меня знают, считают, что я родилась такой.

**Паймон:** ...

**Паймон:** Ведьма... зелье... Прямо как в сказке!

**Сиджвин:** Поверь, это было взаправду.

**Паймон:** Хм. Наверное, дело в том, как ты рассказываешь эту историю...

Сиджвин, ты очень добрая.

**Паймон:** Да, именно так. Не каждая мелюзина примет человеческий облик, чтобы спасти друга. Это серьёзное решение, да?

**Сиджвин:** Лечить пациентов - задача врача, и для этого мы должны искать способы преодолеть любые трудности.

**Паймон:** Я не понимаю, как можно было отказываться от помощи на том лишь основании, что врач - мелюзина. Особенно когда дочь больна...

**Сиджвин:** Ну... Иногда внешность сама по себе является барьером. Но это было давно, и мне нравится, как я выгляжу сейчас.

**Паймон:** Да! Ты очень милая!

**Кальканья:** Уважаемые покупатели! Спасибо всем за ожидание. От имени нашего партнёра по уходу за кожей я рада сообщить, что наш новый потрясающий продукт официально поступил в продажу!

**Сиджвин:** Кажется, началась акция. Пойдёмте посмотрим. Паймон, если тебе нужны средства по уходу за кожей, я могу помочь с выбором.

**Паймон:** Правда? Советы от одного из главных лиц Ассоциации красоты... Превосходно!

**Кальканья:** Наш новый косметический продукт называется «Роса бабочки». Уверена, многие из вас читали о клинических испытаниях этого средства по уходу за кожей.

**Кальканья:** Считается, что оно существенно улучшает качество кожи, а своё название получило благодаря сиянию, которое оно дарит коже. Её можно сравнить с бабочкой, только что претерпевшей метаморфозу и впервые расправляющей свои крылышки.

**Кальканья:** Сегодня мы имеем честь видеть у себя в гостях создателя этого фантастического средства по уходу за кожей, господина Равата из Исследовательского института Фонтейна. Давайте поприветствуем. Он подробно расскажет об этом продукте!

**Рават:** Благодарю вас. Здравствуйте, я Рават, создатель «Росы бабочки».

**Истрес:** Какой красавчик!

**Орильяк:** Исследователь? А по нему и не скажешь...

**Рават:** Ха-ха, кажется, все удивлены моим появлением. Или лучше сказать, моей внешностью?

**Рават:** Несомненно, многие считают нас, исследователей, книжными червями. В этом отношении я действительно выгляжу несколько необычно.

**Рават:** На самом деле внешность всего лишь способ оценки человека. Однако она может превратиться в самые настоящие оковы, мешающие в жизни.

**Рават:** Она способна лишить молодого человека уверенности в том, что он получит работу, или похитить у девушки смелость признаться в любви. В итоге мы становимся людьми, которые всё больше соответствуют тому, какими нас видят окружающие.

**Паймон:** Хм...

**Рават:** Но что, если я скажу вам, что эти оковы можно снять?

**Рават:** Если внешность мешает нам измениться, то почему бы нам не изменить саму внешность!

**Рават:** Сотрите тёмные пятна и морщины, и они больше не лишат нас уверенности в себе. Придайте коже сияние и блеск, и мы не будем дрожать ни перед кем.

**Рават:** Именно для этого и существует «Роса бабочки». Её предназначение - помочь нам выбраться из кокона и начать совершенно новую, наполненную преобразованиями жизнь!

**Паймон:** Вау! Паймон не знает, что там за косметика и какими эффектами она обладает, но говорит он очень убедительно!

**Истрес:** Господин Рават, позвольте узнать, вы сами пользовались этим продуктом?

**Рават:** Разумеется. Я много раз пробовал его во время тестирования, поэтому можете считать меня амбассадором продукта.

**Паймон:** Тогда эффект гарантирован!

**Рават:** Понимаю, что это выглядит как обычное хвастовство. Могу ли я пригласить добровольца, чтобы он опробовал средство на себе?

**Паймон:** Паймон! Паймон хочет попробовать!

**Рават:** Ох, ну...

**Рават:** Какая честь! Не ожидал, что к нам присоединится мэтр Ассоциации красоты. В таком случае, госпожа Сиджвин, не могли бы мы узнать ваше экспертное мнение об этом продукте?

**Сиджвин:** Хм... Конечно.

**Истрес:** Это Ромарин? Говорят, что во всём Фонтейне не найти более придирчивую потребительницу средств по уходу за кожей.

**Рават:** Пожалуйста, попробуйте.

**Сиджвин:** Хм... Средство очень приятное, быстро впитывается... Это первоклассный продукт даже по стандартам Фонтейна.

**Паймон:** Ого, Сиджвин дала наивысшую оценку.

**Покупатели:** Я хочу купить! И я тоже! А скидки предусмотрены?

**Рават:** Тогда не будем заставлять вас ждать. «Роса бабочки» уже в продаже!

*Восторженные покупатели быстро опустошают запасы.*

**Паймон:** Путешественник, может, тоже купим?

- **Путешественник:** Паймон, ты и так симпатичная.

- **Путешественник:** Ты сама за него заплатишь?

**Паймон:** Правда? Но за кожей ведь всё равно нужно ухаживать? Паймон считает, что её кожа огрубела от всех этих путешествий на свежем воздухе...

**Паймон:** Так всё дело в море, да? Ладно, в этот раз Паймон заплатит! И поделится с тобой. Паймон здорово придумала, да?

**Паймон:** Не думай о том, подойдёт тебе средство или нет, ведь с нами Сиджвин, эксперт по вопросам красоты.

#### Попросите совета у Сиджвин

**Рават:** Для меня большая честь получить от вас такую высокую оценку. Честно говоря, я надеялся услышать о возможных недостатках и рекомендациях по улучшению.

**Сиджвин:** Преимущества и недостатки большинства средств для ухода за кожей можно узнать только после длительного использования.

**Сиджвин:** Но эффект от этого средства проявился мгновенно и не вызвал никаких явных проблем, при этом улучшив качество кожи. Такое нечасто случается.

**Сиджвин:** Не волнуйтесь, я продолжу тестировать его и регулярно публиковать оценки в «Красоте сегодня». Вы сможете прочитать их, если подпишетесь.

**Рават:** Хе-хе... Я бы предпочёл, чтобы эта беседа была менее формальной, но, полагаю, таковы строгие стандарты мэтра Ассоциации красоты.

**Паймон:** Сиджвин! Гм... Уважаемый эксперт по красоте! Как вы думаете, подойдёт ли нам этот продукт?

**Рават:** О, неужели это (прославленная)(прославленный) Путешественник?

**Паймон:** Да, и не менее прославленная Паймон тоже здесь.

**Рават:** Ха-ха... Раз у вас возник такой вопрос, позвольте мне лично ответить.

**Рават:** «Роса бабочки» - настолько хорошее средство, что им можно смело пользоваться вне зависимости от того, какой у вас тип кожи. Если и есть какая-то разница в эффективности, то я бы сказал, что у нас, жителей Фонтейна, она проявляется чуть лучше.

**Паймон:** Почему это?

**Рават:** Боюсь, дорогая госпожа Паймон, вам придётся позволить мне сохранить некоторые коммерческие тайны.

**Паймон:** Хм-м... Ладно! А как насчёт специальной цены для нас?

**Рават:** Для меня будет большой честью, если вы воспользуетесь моим продуктом. Вот, возьмите эти два флакона совершенно бесплатно.

**Паймон:** Хе-хе, Паймон снова удалось сэкономить!

Похоже на какой-то маркетинговый ход...

**Рават:** Ха-ха, это можно воспринимать и так. Тем не менее это подарок от чистого сердца, не стоит относиться к нему лишь с деловой точки зрения.

**Рават:** Поиск покупателей для «Росы бабочки» сродни поиску соратников на пути к красоте. Это доставляет мне истинное удовольствие.

**Паймон:** Путь к красоте? Ух, как-то... неловко слышать такое...

**Рават:** Не нужно стыдиться, госпожа Паймон. Многие говорят, что стремятся к внутренней красоте, но их взоры всегда обращены к красоте внешней.

**Рават:** В этом мире желание быть красивым абсолютно нормально. Именно это и вдохновило меня на выбор данного направления исследований!

**Паймон:** Да, ты прав! Любовь к красоте присуща каждому!

**Рават:** Прошу прощения, но уже поздно, а у меня ещё есть другие дела. Вот, возьмите. С нетерпением жду новой встречи с вами.

**Сиджвин:** Паймон, Путешественник, не спешите использовать это средство.

**Паймон:** Хм? С ним что-то не так?

**Сиджвин:** С качеством всё в порядке, но я полагаю, что его можно улучшить. Думаю, я смогу сделать его эффективнее, добавив несколько ингредиентов.

**Паймон:** Правда? Тогда... Почему ты не сказала об этом Равату?

**Сиджвин:** Исследователи - особый тип людей.

**Сиджвин:** Скажите им в лицо о недостатках, и они не станут возражать. Но стоит указать на то, что кто-то мог бы сделать работу лучше... Это точно вызовет недовольство.

**Сиджвин:** Если бы я сказала об этом, он, вероятно, не дал бы вам два флакона бесплатно.

**Паймон:** Хм, в этом что-то есть.

**Сиджвин:** Если хотите получить улучшенную версию, я могу написать список ингредиентов. Приходите, когда соберёте их.

**Паймон:** Улучшенная версия? Ты имеешь в виду, что Паймон может стать ещё красивее? Хе-хе, тогда это точно нужно сделать!

- **Путешественник:** Ещё красивее?

- **Путешественник:** Ну, раз ты этого хочешь...

**Сиджвин:** А мне пора возвращаться в крепость. Лазарет не может долго оставаться без меня.

**Паймон:** Увидимся в крепости Меропид!

#### Отправляйтесь в крепость Меропид

**Паймон:** Ого, здесь так много людей...

**Эрик:** Пожалуйста, поверь! Я видел его собственными глазами прошлой ночью! Это была моя точная копия! Один в один!

**Сиджвин:** Хорошо. Я всё подробно записала. Теперь мне нужно осмотреть тебя. Дыши глубже. Не волнуйся.

**Сиджвин:** Какой палец я показываю?

**Эрик:** Указательный... Хватит уже, у меня всё в порядке с головой!

**Сиджвин:** Хорошо, продолжим.

**Паймон:** Давно не виделись, герцог!

**Ризли:** А, это вы. И правда. Я бы угостил вас чаем, но, боюсь, меня ждут служебные обязанности.

**Паймон:** Не нужно извиняться. Вообще-то, мы пришли к Сиджвин. Можно поинтересоваться, что здесь происходит?

**Ризли:** Этот заключённый прибежал к стражникам в панике, утверждая, что видел «другого себя».

**Паймон:** Другого себя? Может, они просто похожи?

**Ризли:** Я тоже так думал, когда отчёт впервые попал ко мне на стол. Но мы проверили записи, никто даже отдалённо не похож на него.

**Ризли:** Учитывая его уверенность в увиденном, я могу предположить, что у него какие-то проблемы с психикой, поэтому его осматривает старшая медсестра.

**Сиджвин:** Вот так, хорошо. Попей воды и отдохни.

**Сиджвин:** Осмотр окончен. Я не нашла никаких явных признаков болезни.

**Ризли:** Понятно. Итак, ты по-прежнему считаешь, что проблема скорее в его сознании, чем в теле?

**Сиджвин:** Это всего лишь моё предположение. Психические проблемы бывает трудно выявить. Пока что с уверенностью можно сказать только одно - его восприятие в полном порядке.

**Ризли:** Если об этом станет известно, могут пойти самые разные слухи. Стражники, переведите его в больничную палату на время наблюдения.

**Ризли:** Сумеречный двор попросил меня о содействии, поэтому я не смогу заняться этим делом. Прости за доставленные неудобства, старшая медсестра, но тебе предстоит разобраться с этим делом самостоятельно.

**Сиджвин:** Не беспокойтесь, ваша светлость. Я внесу его в свой список подопечных.

**Ризли:** Кстати, они проделали путь сюда только ради того, чтобы увидеть тебя. Полагаю, на то были веские причины. Но не стоит включать их в свой список.

**Сиджвин:** Ой! Путешественник и Паймон!

**Паймон:** Привет, Сиджвин! Не так уж это и важно, но...

**Ризли:** Я буду в своём кабинете, если возникнут срочные вопросы. С вашего позволения.

**Паймон:** Хорошо, пока-пока!

**Паймон:** Эм, Сиджвин...

**Сиджвин:** Не могли бы вы немного подождать? На очереди ещё один пациент. Устраивайтесь поудобнее.

**Паймон:** Да, конечно.

**Одилон:** Сиджвин, мы его привели. Похоже, его состояние снова ухудшилось.

**Сиджвин:** Не волнуйтесь, пускай пока посидит здесь.

**Паймон:** Паймон и подумать не могла, что она бывает так занята. Может, сейчас не время просить её улучшить средство по уходу за кожей...

- **Путешественник:** Спокойствие, Паймон. Сиджвин обещала нам.

- **Путешественник:** А давай посмотрим, можем ли мы ей помочь?

Таинственное и запутанное дело, связанное с косметикой, теперь полностью раскрыто. После того как Сиджвин помогла Сумеречному двору распутать дело, она готова рассказать вам историю из прошлого...

**Паймон:** С другой стороны, это может быть последний флакон во всём Тейвате. Как-то не очень хочется его выбрасывать. Надо попытаться придать ему какой-то особый смысл... О, Паймон знает!

**Паймон:** Строение мелюзин не изменилось в результате великого потопа, поэтому ты и дальше сможешь испытывать на себе действие зелья. В таком случае не хочешь вернуть свой прежний облик?

**Паймон:** В конце концов мелюзины больше нравятся другим мелюзинам, так ведь?

Паймон...

**Сиджвин:** Паймон, какая разница, человек я или мелюзина?

**Паймон:** Эм...

**Паймон:** О чём вообще Паймон думала? Ты ведь уже давно приняла решение. Хорошо. Давайте уничтожим его прямо здесь и сейчас.

**Сиджвин:** Я этим займусь.

**Paimon:** Oh, frogs, huh...

**Sigewinne:** Mm-hmm. And we're done. All that's left now is to pack them into lunchboxes and hand them to Wolsey.

**Sigewinne:** Yesterday in the production zone, I noticed an inmate who's been working for two days straight. That means it's a healthy meal for him tomorrow.

**Paimon:** Sigewinne really does have the inmates' best interests at heart.

#### Give the prepared ingredients to Wolsey

**Wolsey:** Ah, Head Nurse! Got some extras for me?

**Sigewinne:** Just the one today. The serial number's on the lid.

**Wolsey:** Sounds good! I'll make sure it gets delivered.

**Paimon:** Sounds like they're talking in code.

**Sigewinne:** Thanks for your help.

**Wolsey:** Oh, it's no trouble at all. I only wish I could do more. You work so hard for all our sakes... I'd happily lose a little sleep if it meant helping you out.

**Sigewinne:** That won't do. If you reduce your sleeping hours any further, your health will start to suffer.

**Wolsey:** Hmm... Alright, nurse's orders — I'll do as I'm told. But you take care of yourself too.

**Sigewinne:** Well, I'm done with work for today. Thanks for helping out, you two.

**Paimon:** Seems like the guards and staff here have a lot of respect for you, Sigewinne. In just one day of helping you with your work, it feels like we've done a lot for the Fortress.

**Courbevoie:** Head Nurse! Please, wait! I'm sorry to disturb you this late at night, but I think you'll agree this is urgent.

**Sigewinne:** Okay, just relax and catch your breath. What's the matter?

**Courbevoie:** The guard at the observation ward, Odilon, he told me to pass on a message to you. One of the inmates there, the guy suffering from possible hallucinations... Well, he seems to have made a sudden recovery. And now he wants to apply for permission to leave the ward.

**Paimon:** Huh? Already?

**Sigewinne:** You were right to tell me. I'll have to examine him and make sure he's well before we discharge him.

**Paimon:** Looks like we've got one job as medical assistants left. We'll come with you.

#### Go to the observation ward

**Enri:** Ah, Head Nurse, you're finally here. I'm so sorry for the all trouble I've caused you, but I believe I've now made a full recovery.

**Enri:** After giving it some thought, I'm sure that I was probably just seeing things. Stress-related, I imagine. I'd been working long hours, so I definitely wasn't in the best state of mind.

**Sigewinne:** I totally understand. Still, I have to give you an examination before I can discharge you, okay? So take a deep breath, and don't be nervous. It'll be the exact same procedure as we did this morning. We can start whenever you're ready.

**Enri:** Uhh... Again, so sorry — I was in such a state this morning, I barely recall anything... Would you mind taking me through it again?

**Sigewinne:** Not at all, it's a lot to remember. First up, we have a little cognitive test. Just concentrate, and you'll be fine. To start with — which finger am I holding up?

**Enri:** Your pinky.

*Under Sigewinne's guidance, Erick quickly completes the full series of tests.*

**Sigewinne:** Alright, all indicators seem normal.

**Paimon:** So it was a stress-induced hallucination after all?

**Sigewinne:** Well, we've certainly had previous cases of mental illness caused by overwork, which is why I carefully observe everyone in the production zone. I wonder how I managed to miss the signs this time...?

**Enri:** Please, Head Nurse, you shouldn't blame yourself! I'm just still adapting, that's all. Pushed myself too hard and I guess I couldn't take it.

**Enri:** Thanks for looking after me. I'll be as good as new after a good night's sleep, and if you can give me the okay, I know I'll sleep better back in my good old dorm...

**Sigewinne:** It can be difficult to relax in an unfamiliar environment... Alright then, you can leave the ward.

**Sigewinne:** I suggest you take two days off to focus on your recovery. I'll take you off the observation list for now, so you're officially discharged.

**Enri:** Great. I can't thank you enough, Head Nurse.

**Sigewinne:** Mr. Odilon, could you escort him back?

**Lazare:** Of course. I'm on guard duty in the Administrative Area tonight, so nothing will go wrong. You have my word.

**Paimon:** Guess you're finally done for the day now, huh?

**Sigewinne:** Yep... Ah, but I still haven't gotten around to making that improved lotion for you yet.

**Paimon:** No rush! Seriously, it's super late now. You should go get some rest too!

**Sigewinne:** Tomorrow it is, then. Good night, Traveler. Good night, Paimon. Hope you sleep well.

**Paimon:** Oh, don't you worry, we will! After a productive day of work, we're both gonna sleep like a log!

Good night, Sigewinne.

**Paimon:** Yeah, sleep tight, Sigewinne! See ya tomorrow!

### Switcheroo

After Sigewinne finally finishes a busy day as head nurse, it seems as though a new problem has already arrived...

#### Meet with Sigewinne

**Paimon:** Whoa... How is the infirmary even busier than yesterday?

**Sigewinne:** Good morning, Traveler, Paimon. I have a few people here for a checkup, so you might need to wait for a while.

**Paimon:** No problem, anything we can do to help?

**Sigewinne:** Umm... There's a bit of a complicated situation here, I'm still getting to the bottom of it myself...

**Houilles:** You have to believe me... I'm telling the truth!

**Wriothesley:** ...

**Lambersart:** You're in a medical facility. Keep it down!

**Houilles:** Please, listen to me! I'm not Gascon, I'm Houilles!

**Morgane:** You're a horrible liar, you know that? We get all your mugshots with the arrest warrants. It's clear as day that you're Gascon.

**Houilles:** You mean because of the way I look? I don't know what happened, I just woke up like this, then you guys dragged me in here for an interrogation...

**Wriothesley:** I brought you here to figure out what's going on. If you truly are the victim of an injustice, yelling isn't going to achieve anything — at most, it'll delay your vindication.

**Houilles:** ...Alright, I get it.

**Morgane:** This is nuts. Yesterday's interrogations went fine, and now this...

**Paimon:** Morning, Your Grace. What's going on?

**Wriothesley:** Remember the case I mentioned yesterday? The one brought to me by the Marechaussee Phantom? Well, we've been questioning the inmates involved this morning. Every single one of them is claiming mistaken identity.

**Paimon:** Huh... That's even weirder than what that guy was saying yesterday...

**Wriothesley:** The thing is, none of them have been able to provide any evidence whatsoever to support their claims. Questioning them further got us nowhere, so for now we've brought them to the infirmary.

**Paimon:** And we thought yesterday was as busy as it got for Sigewinne...

**Morgane:** We've compiled their statements. Have a look. Perhaps there's something we missed.

**Paimon:** Wait...

**Sigewinne:** Hmm...

**Wriothesley:** "Release"? Interesting. The inmates they spoke of in their statements appear to all be due for release today.

**Morgane:** I noticed that too... If that's their plan, they're underestimating us a little. Every serious offender claims they're someone else who just so happens to be due for release today... And they're expecting us to go along with that?

**Wriothesley:** All the more reason to be cautious. Lambersart, check the release list for today, and make sure no one gets processed. We're gonna need an excuse, though... Just... say we're out of forms. And that we're furiously printing more as we speak.

**Lambersart:** I'm on it!

**Wriothesley:** It's conceivable that our group of serious offenders somehow found a way to switch places with inmates who are about to be released, so they can walk out right under our noses.

**Morgane:** We did consider that possibility, but I mean... Just look at them. They match the warrants, period. How could there have been a switch?

**Wriothesley:** Only if they're dead ringers for the inmates due for release, I suppose... I gave you that name list yesterday to assist in your investigation. Did that help?

**Morgane:** Yes, the inmates' mugshots were attached to the list. The first thing I did was check for lookalikes, and I didn't find any.

**Morgane:** The only other thing that stands out to me is that their voices sound a little different today. But it's not exactly hard to put on a fake voice.

**Wriothesley:** Oh, really? Hmm... What does our head nurse make of all this?

**Sigewinne:** Give me a moment. I think I have a way to confirm whether there's been a switch.

**Paimon:** Huh? Isn't that the guy from yesterday?

**Sigewinne:** This should be Honore. He has very severe facial ulcers, which require an operation in the hospital at the Court of Fontaine. I authorized him to leave the Fortress for medical treatment under guarded escort.

**Honore:** Yes, like I told you! I'm not Potton! I'm Honore!

**Morgane:** So there has been a swap... Well, I can see what happened in Potton's case, but how do you explain the other inmates? You're not telling me their... souls switched bodies, are you?

**Sigewinne:** No. I believe it was their faces that got switched.

**Sigewinne:** The method is a bit like replacing a tooth: Take the bad tooth out, put a false one in. Hmm... And actually, I think I can prove it to you.

**Sigewinne:** Traveler, Paimon, do you remember Erick from the other day?

**Paimon:** Yeah, sure do!

**Sigewinne:** Then you can be my witnesses. To start with, please fetch the inmate who claims to be Erick.

**Sigewinne:** I know, it's not time to change your dressing yet, but please bear with me.

#### Find the suspect who claims to be Erick

**Lambersart:** Your Grace! Bad news — I paused the releases like you asked, but we were too late... a bunch of people already got processed two hours ago.

**Lambersart:** I checked the list, and sure enough... it's all the people who gave statements.

**Wriothesley:** Two hours ago? That's before today's interrogations began... They had this all planned out.

**Morgane:** So there really was a switch after all? Ugh... They really pulled the wool over our eyes on this one.

**Paimon:** We should be able to catch up with them if we leave now, right?

**Morgane:** Not if that potion is real. They can just switch faces again after leaving the Fortress. And if the Marechaussee's Melusines can't even spot them, we wouldn't know where to start searching.

**Paimon:** Yikes... so we're at a complete dead end!

**Ceasth:** Well... Technically, there is one more lead we could follow up on.

**Ceasth:** We were only able to arrest this gang thanks to the help of a researcher from the Fontaine Research Institute. I believe his name is Rawat.

**Sigewinne:** Oh?

**Paimon:** Rawat? Isn't that the handsome guy we met at that skincare promotion?

**Ceasth:** Handsome? Maybe from a human point of view. I just remember that he specialized in potions.

**Ceasth:** One of the crimes this gang is charged with is the manufacture and sale of illicit drugs. According Mr. Rawat, they appear to have stolen technology he developed through his research, and used it in their operation.

**Ceasth:** At first, he thought it was a typical case of intellectual property infringement, and he tried to negotiate with them. But once he discovered their criminal operation, he reported them to the authorities.

**Ceasth:** Thanks to his report, we were able to swoop in and arrest them all in one go.

**Paimon:** What a hero. Bet that took courage on his part.

**Wriothesley:** No doubt. This is worth following up on. As the one who snitched on them, it's highly likely that Rawat will be targeted by the escapees.

**Wriothesley:** That aside, since he's negotiated with them in person before, there's a chance he'll have some additional information for us.

**Morgane:** Understood, Your Grace. My team and I will head to the Fontaine Research Institute right away. In the meantime, please keep an eye on the face-switch victims for us.

**Wriothesley:** Of course.

**Sigewinne:** Um, Miss Morgane? I'd like to come along and help you catch these criminals.

**Morgane:** Why's that?

**Sigewinne:** Well, I'm the one who discharged the fake Erick last night, so I feel partly responsible.

**Morgane:** Don't say that, Head Nurse. Your focus was on the inmates' health, and rightly so. We can't ask you to help with arresting criminals — that's our duty.

**Sigewinne:** Umm... I also have a more personal reason. Potton is a patient of mine, and if I don't get a new batch of medicine to him in time, his condition will get much worse.

**Morgane:** Look... Head Nurse, I completely understand where you're coming from. But arresting criminals is dangerous business, and if anything happened to you... None of us can afford to take on that responsibility.

**Sigewinne:** Don't worry, I can defend myself just fine.

**Morgane:** You say that, but still—

**Wriothesley:** If I may, Ms. Morgane — Sigewinne is the one who raised the potion hypothesis, and I'm sure she has far more expertise on the topic than your team. My suggestion would be to bring her with you.

**Wriothesley:** And if you really are worried for her safety, then...

Paimon and I are coming along anyway.

**Wriothesley:** Ah, there you go, right on cue. Knew I could count on you.

**Morgane:** Okay... Well, since this plan has the Duke's blessing, far be it from me to refuse... We should head off immediately. Time is of the essence.

#### Go to the Fontaine Research Institute

**Morgane:** Greetings. Sorry to impose, but I'm afraid we're here on urgent business, and we're gonna need you to cooperate.

**Bognor:** The Marechaussee Phantom? Let me guess, you're investigating some more illegal research?

**Morgane:** Not this time, no. We're looking for a researcher, do you know where we might find Mr. Rawat?

**Bognor:** Rawat... Oh, yeah — Mr. Handsome, right?

**Paimon:** Just a handful? Paimon figured he'd be the most popular guy around.

**Bognor:** Well, I guess you could say he's popular, at least on a superficial level. He's blessed with good looks, so he tends to make a good first impression on people.

**Bognor:** But in all his time here at the Fontaine Research Institute, I don't think he's ever had a single deep conversation about his research with his peers. That's no way to make real friends around here.

**Paimon:** That's so weird... He definitely didn't come across like a recluse at that product promotion.

**Sigewinne:** I don't find that weird at all. He definitely didn't strike me as the kind of guy who's good at making friends.

**Paimon:** Really? Paimon couldn't tell... must be a Melusine perception thing.

**Morgane:** Okay — we should talk with his acquaintances, but we also need to check the research institute for any clues. Let's split up, it'll save us some time.

**Paimon:** Sure thing. We'll go ask around among the other researchers with Sigewinne. Paimon's suddenly kinda curious to know what everyone really thinks of him.

**Bognor:** He actually doesn't work on site here very often. There's a handful researchers in the nearby area who know him pretty well, though. You could see what they know.

#### Gather intelligence from researchers familiar with Rawat

**Paimon:** Hey there! Are you Antibes?

**Antibes:** What do you want?

**Paimon:** We're here to ask about Rawat. Any idea where he might be? We were told you're pretty close with him.

**Antibes:** "Close"? Hmph, not sure that's the word I'd use. He wouldn't even let me use his patent.

**Paimon:** ...Huh?

**Antibes:** It's been a good few days since I last saw him. I don't know where he's at. Go ask someone else.

**Sigewinne:** Umm... Would the patent in question happen to be related to the new skincare product he developed?

**Antibes:** Tsk... Yeah, kinda. "Butterfly Dew," or whatever he calls it. Hmph, I heard he made a tidy sum off of that one...

**Paimon:** Okay, now we're getting somewhere! Keep going...

**Antibes:** Most of the other researchers here focus on mechanics, energy, stuff like that. I get why he wouldn't be interested in having anything to do with them.

**Antibes:** But I'm studying coatings. There's a huge overlap in our areas of research! Why would he refuse to work with me?

**Antibes:** I bought his product once and noticed it contains a unique substance that might have applications in coatings too. If it worked out, I could probably make as much as he's making.

**Antibes:** Don't get me wrong, I had no intention of plagiarizing his work. I was hoping to get official permission to use his findings in my own research. Followed the proper procedure, it was all above-board — I would've had to pay him a license fee and everything.

**Antibes:** But despite that, he turned me down not once, not twice, but three times! It's like he's vehemently opposed to the idea of anyone else making the kind of money he does!

**Paimon:** Huh, so is Rawat antisocial, or is this guy just jealous?

Maybe both of the above...

**Paimon:** Well, doesn't look like we're gonna get any info about Rawat's whereabouts from him. Let's go.

**Sigewinne:** Wait, one second... Mr. Antibes? Next time you go shopping, you should buy some Bulle Fruits. Peel the skin, steep in water, then drink — it'll help calm your mood.

**Antibes:** Huh? Uhh, yeah, thanks...

#### Gather intelligence from researchers familiar with Rawat

**Paimon:** Excuse us, are you Audree?

**Audree:** That's me. What is it?

**Paimon:** Are you close friends with Rawat? We're trying to find out where he is.

**Audree:** Close friends? Pff. You mean, because I told him I had feelings for him once? I doubt I'm the first or last at the institute to make that mistake... Why would you single me out as a "close friend"?

**Audree:** I should've known, really. Men like him, they just enjoy being surrounded by fawning women... They're not interested in a serious relationship.

**Paimon:** Oookay... Um, we were just kinda wondering if you might know where we can find him...

**Audree:** Why would I know where he's at? I barely know him, and he probably doesn't even remember me.

**Paimon:** Ugh, this is getting annoying. Shall we go ask someone else?

**Sigewinne:** Hmm, I think she knows Rawat better than she cares to admit. If we leave now, it might be a wasted opportunity.

**Paimon:** Maybe, but we're not going to get much out of her while she's in this state, are we?

**Sigewinne:** She's displaying symptoms common among people in love, if that's what you mean. But I know a cure.

**Paimon:** ...It's curable?

**Sigewinne:** *clears throat* We're still no closer to finding Mr. Rawat... By this point, he could be in grave danger.

**Audree:** Danger? Wait, why are you looking for him, again?

**Paimon:** Ohh... That's right, yeah! The Marechaussee Phantom said that he's been targeted by some pretty bad people. We need to make sure he's safe.

**Audree:** What? How has this happened!? He seemed fine when I last saw him, and that was only a couple of days ago... Just happened to run into him, of course.

**Sigewinne:** I see, I see. Well, do you have any thoughts on where he might be right now?

**Audree:** ...Okay, but this is purely out of concern for his safety... If he's not at the Institute, he might be at his mother's place.

**Audree:** I heard she raised him alone, that they had it pretty tough financially, and these days she's housebound due to illness. He makes regular trips back home to check on her.

**Paimon:** Ooh, okay. Do you know the address?

**Audree:** Nope, sorry. I only found out the thing about his mother because it came up in conversation once. I was just trying to find out more about him. Y'know, so I could get to know him better.

**Audree:** When I told him I had a crush on him, I offered to help him take care of his mother. And yeah, maybe that was a stupid idea, but did he really have to yell at me for it? It's like, fine, I can take no for an answer, but what's with the temper tantrum?

**Audree:** Unbelievable. Ugh, these guys only wanna show you their charming, well-groomed exterior. They can't stand it when a few home truths shine through...

**Paimon:** Looks like her love syndrome is flaring up again...

**Sigewinne:** We need to find out Mr. Rawat's mother's address... Let's check with the Marechaussee officers when we regroup.

**Paimon:** Cool, sounds like a plan.

#### Gather intelligence from researchers familiar with Rawat

**Paimon:** Sorry to bother you, Sir, but are you Bertram?

**Bertram:** Who's asking?

**Paimon:** Just some concerned citizens helping out the Marechaussee Phantom with an investigation. We'd like to ask you about Rawat's whereabouts, if that's okay. We're told the two of you are pretty close?

**Bertram:** Not anymore, we're not. Turns out we don't see eye to eye.

**Paimon:** Oh yeah? What happened?

**Bertram:** *sigh* ...The thing about us researchers is, most of us do what we do for some sort of higher purpose. But not Rawat. All he cared about was how to commercialize his findings.

**Bertram:** He won't even let you work with him unless you sign a confidentiality agreement first. All so he has better control over who gets which intellectual property rights. How are you supposed to get any meaningful research done working with a guy like that?

**Bertram:** Now that I've got my own technical solution for my compressor, I don't need to work with him anymore.

**Paimon:** So that's what Rawat is really like...

**Sigewinne:** We'd still like to know where to find him. Is that something you can help us with?

**Bertram:** Wait, you're... Yeah, I know you, you're the head nurse at the Fortress of Meropide. Alright, then I'm happy to help you if I can. I respect the work you do — medicine serves the highest purpose of all the sciences.

**Paimon:** Wow, turns out Sigewinne's got some real clout.

**Bertram:** Thing is, I have no real way of knowing where he might be. I stopped working with him two months ago, and I've barely seen him since.

**Bertram:** But if you want my advice, I'd say go talk to the peddlers. There's a few he meets with every other day or so, maybe they know where he's at.

**Sigewinne:** Peddlers?

**Bertram:** Yeah. Back when I was working with him, I noticed he met with them quite often. Wouldn't surprise me if they were involved in some shady business together, because it seemed like they went out of their way to avoid discussing anything in my presence.

**Bertram:** The little I overheard was about manufacturing and selling pharmaceutical drugs. Like I said, he's all about the money.

**Paimon:** Drugs? Doesn't he work with skincare products? Do those count as drugs?

**Bertram:** Who knows? I never cared to find out. He's the Mora-grubber, not me.

**Sigewinne:** Thank you. You've given us plenty to work with.

**Bertram:** Welcome. Goodbye, and may we both reach new heights in our scientific endeavors.

#### Rendezvous with the Marechaussee Phantom officers

**Paimon:** Y'know, Paimon started out thinking Rawat was a handsome researcher with a friendly and cheerful personality. It's such a surprise to learn that his peers have a different impression of him.

**Sigewinne:** So what's your view of him now, Paimon?

**Paimon:** Hmm... A handsome researcher whose difficult upbringing turned him into a profit-chaser with a chip on his shoulder...?

**Sigewinne:** So your thoughts about his appearance haven't changed at all.

**Paimon:** Well... His looks are kind of a big deal, at least in Paimon's opinion. How does such a handsome guy end up being so antisocial? Even with his difficult family background, it just doesn't make any sense.

**Sigewinne:** You can't judge a person solely based on their appearance.

**Morgane:** Head Nurse, Traveler, I assume you've spoken with the researchers? What did you find out?

*You exchange the intelligence you've gathered.*

**Sigewinne:** Bertram mentioned Rawat's involvement with some pharmaceutical merchants. Do you know if he's registered any pharmaceutical brands?

**Ceasth:** Since he was the whistleblower on such a huge case, I ran a background check on him. Hmm... I don't remember there being any brands under his name.

**Sigewinne:** How strange... If he was so focused on commercializing his research and averse to sharing his technology with others, why wouldn't he have registered his own brands?

**Sigewinne:** Maybe he and his "business partners" needed to avoid public scrutiny?

**Ceasth:** You think he may have colluded with the gang? Hmm... Well, the crime was manufacture and sale of drugs, and that would certainly be in his wheelhouse. We can't rule it out.

**Paimon:** Isn't that pretty unlikely though? He was the one who blew the whistle on them.

**Sigewinne:** Most children who come to me with tooth decay have suffered pain for a long time already, but they'll still try to hide the fact that they eat too many sweets. Grown-ups and children aren't so different in that regard.

**Paimon:** Hmm, if you say so...

**Morgane:** I saw him when he came to make his report, and I certainly didn't get the feeling that he was the type to collude with criminals. Perhaps it's more likely that they were coercing him?

**Morgane:** You mentioned that he comes from a poor family. So it's possible that the criminals approached him asking for academic support, luring him in with the promise of riches.

**Morgane:** By the time he discovered who they really were, he was in too deep, they had leverage on him, so he was forced to keep working for them... I've seen it happen many times before.

**Paimon:** That does make a lot of sense. It would definitely explain why he was so unwilling to cooperate with his fellow researchers.

**Paimon:** No wonder he's so unsociable. He must be on edge all the time with a gang of crooks breathing down his neck.

**Sigewinne:** In that case, the situation could be worse than we thought.

**Sigewinne:** If Rawat was working with the escaped inmates, they're sure to know more about his whereabouts than us. There's a good chance they'll get to him before we do.

**Ceasth:** Based on the information we've gathered so far, he was last seen two days ago. If no one's seen him since, maybe we're... too late...

**Paimon:** Two days ago? So he went missing right after that product promotion?

**Morgane:** That was the same day he reported the criminals, too. Not a good sign.

**Sigewinne:** Hmmm... Don't worry, everyone. I believe we still have a chance. Since he disappeared right after blowing the whistle, it's likely that he's gone into hiding.

**Paimon:** Yeah. He must've been worried that they'd come for him.

**Morgane:** All the more reason to find him as soon as possible. The only lead we have at the moment is his mother's house, which you mentioned earlier. Ceasth, think you can find the place?

**Ceasth:** Yep. *chuckle* When you register a brand, the Court of Fontaine requires you to provide a legal address. The one Rawat wrote down was in the outskirts of the city. That should be the place.

**Sigewinne:** Okay, then there's no time to lose. Let's move!

**Morgane:** Got it... Okay, I'll tell the squad members in hiding to stay put and keep a lookout.

**Sigewinne:** Thanks for helping me out yet again, Traveler and Paimon.

**Paimon:** No problem! But, when you say her condition is serious... Do you mean...?

**Sigewinne:** Yes. She is barely clinging on to life.

**Paimon:** Aw...

**Sigewinne:** Let me take care of a few things first. Could you pass me the towel over there?

**Paimon:** Yeah, of course!

**Sigewinne:** I can tell that her family has tried a lot of different treatment methods, including some very expensive medicines. Unfortunately, her condition is so multifaceted that a recovery looks all but impossible.

**Paimon:** Sigewinne... Paimon bets being a nurse must be pretty tough at times like this?

**Sigewinne:** Yes... It makes me a little sad.

**Sigewinne:** In a Melusine's eyes, a human's birth, aging, sickness, and death are as much part of natural law as sunrise and sunset.

**Sigewinne:** But human medicine can defy that law to some extent. In that sense, it is a science of miracles — that is why I became fascinated with it in the first place.

**Sigewinne:** But it was only after I truly became a doctor that I realized, miracles don't always happen. Even the greatest doctors cannot cure all diseases.

**Paimon:** Sigewinne...

**Sigewinne:** Don't worry about me, Paimon. A doctor's duty is not to cure all diseases, but to treat whatever ailments they can.

**Sigewinne:** At the very least, I can lower her fever. That way, she'll suffer less, and she might even be able to see her son.

**Paimon:** Yeah... All the more reason to find Rawat as soon as possible, even if we don't catch those crooks!

**Sick Old Lady:** *cough*...

**Sigewinne:** Her fever's getting worse. Could I get some hot water?

**Paimon:** Sure, we're on the case!

(Hmm? This cabinet seems to have a hidden compartment.)

#### Talk to Sigewinne

**Paimon:** Sure did! And look what else we found!

**Paimon:** Judging by this journal, it seems like Rawat has a private lab. There's a good chance he could be there right now! We gotta go tell Officer Morgane right away!

**Sigewinne:** That's great news — but hold on just a moment.

**Sigewinne:** The wet towel is ready. I'm giving you another injection... Are you feeling better now?

### Underground Crime

A new clue discovered in Rawat's mother's residence seems to be a critical piece of evidence allowing the investigation to proceed.

#### Go to Rawat's private lab

**Paimon:** It's Officer Morgane, they're already here!

**Morgane:** We came as soon as we heard the news... I can't believe we were so careless. The most crucial information of all was hiding right there, in that room, and we missed it...

**Sigewinne:** Don't blame yourself. Right now, we need to focus on finding a way in.

**Paimon:** There's a mechanical door over here... Hopefully the instructions in Rawat's journal will help us open it.

**Morgane:** It's open. Let's go in.

#### Search deeper

**Lazare:** The Marechaussee Phantom? How did they find this place!?

**Paimon:** Wait... it's that guard from the Fortress...

**Lazare:** That's right, I'm on your side! Uh... I mean, the Duke sent me. What's the matter? Some sort of mixup?

**Sigewinne:** Mr. Odilon, didn't I tell you a few days ago that you need to stop all field work until you've recovered?

**Lazare:** Ah, well, y'know... Gotta help His Grace out, share the load! *coughing*

**Sigewinne:** Officer Morgane, this man is an impostor. The real Mr. Odilon has no recent health issues.

**Lazare:** What? But, I...

**Morgane:** Looks like the Head Nurse was right about the face-swapping method... Arrest them all!

**Morgane:** Dupont, stay here and keep guard — and watch the exit. If we're not back soon, call for reinforcements.

**Dupont:** Got it!

**Dupont:** Captain! According to our list, many of the fugitives aren't accounted for. There may be others hiding deeper inside the facility.

**Morgane:** Ah. So these guys were just the lookout crew...

#### Search deeper

**Paimon:** So many jars. Was this some sort of production facility?

**Sigewinne:** This scent... It's probably the ingredients used to make Butterfly Dew.

**Paimon:** How come they're all empty? Did it sell so well that he ran out of supplies?

**Morgane:** How strange... Why would Rawat choose to manufacture a popular product like that in such a difficult-to-reach location?

**Morgane:** Given our suspicion that he may have been coerced into the illegal drug trade, I would have thought that any production facility we find here would be used for illicit purposes.

**Sigewinne:** Perhaps Butterfly Dew is itself an illegal drug.

**Morgane:** Do you have any evidence for that claim?

**Sigewinne:** No, just a guess. We need to find Rawat as soon as possible. He has all the answers we need.

**Morgane:** Understood. Well, let's not linger here. Let's go, come on!

#### Look for a way to go deeper

**Paimon:** This room's much smaller than the others.

**Sigewinne:** Judging by the implements on the table, this looks like a potion-making lab.

**Morgane:** Head Nurse! Over here!

**Paimon:** Let's see... Huh, Rawat's clothes? Why would he change here?

**Sigewinne:** Hmm... Perhaps they stripped him down, left the clothes behind, and disposed of him somewhere else?

**Paimon:** Yikes, Sigewinne, where did that come from!?

**Morgane:** What a tragic way to go... Especially with such a bright future ahead of him...

**Sigewinne:** There's a path leading further on, Officer Morgane. Let's keep up the pace.

#### Head into the deepest part of the lab

**Paimon:** Shh! There's a whole bunch of people in there!

**Gascon:** Are you serious? You're telling us that within a day, our faces are going to rot like yours?

**Potton:** Fontainian physiology changed dramatically after that great flood, and it's not as compatible with this face-changing solution as before. The side effects are as you can see.

**Gascon:** Why didn't you say anything before we all used it!?

**Potton:** Look. My plan got you all out of prison, didn't it? You'd have much bigger problems to worry about right now if the Marechaussee Phantom found out about your other crimes... Consider an ugly mug getting off lightly.

**Gascon:** Ugh, you're just lucky we didn't leave you in there, after you disappeared on us with no warning! We only let it slide 'cause we're former partners... and now you have the gall to pull a stunt like this?

**Potton:** "Partners"? Make no mistake, each of us is only looking out for himself. If you place too much trust in others, well I'm afraid that's your problem.

**Potton:** You hid things from me, too. Remember how you each had a spare potion, yet kept quiet about it until we planned the prison break?

**Gascon:** Oh, and you didn't have a secret spare? Then do you wanna explain how you melted your face off before all this?

**Enri:** Guys, that's enough, calm down. It is what it is. Arguing won't change anything.

**Enri:** What's the plan, Potton? I'm assuming you brought us here for a reason?

**Gascon:** There is no plan! We followed him here on the promise of more juice, but it turns out there ain't a drop here!

**Gascon:** Besides, what does it matter now anyway? He already said, even if we did get our hands on more of that stuff, switching faces again solves nothing. They're still gonna melt off eventually.

**Enri:** I said, enough arguing! Potton, tell us the plan.

**Potton:** Hmph. The most practical solution is to continue using the potion. Keep switching to a new face before the ulcers can start forming.

**Gascon:** Are you kidding me? This stuff was hard enough to get hold of even when we had a supply. Now you're asking us to use it like skin lotion, after the flood all but wiped the supply out!?

**Potton:** Well, there is another option. But you'll have to make a deal with me first.

**Enri:** Okay, now we're talking. What's the deal?

**Potton:** I have a document I took from that researcher that details how to make a replacement. Just give me one bottle of the undiluted fluid, and I will get to work.

**Enri:** Uhh...

**Potton:** What's the problem? I thought you always kept reserves? Or did you really just have one spare bottle each?

**Gascon:** What do you think? That we were shipping them into the Fortress of Meropide by the basketload?

**Potton:** Well then... say hello to never showing your marred faces in public again!

**Gascon:** Gah, I've heard enough! Get him! Let's see if that document even exists!

**Morgane:** Freeze! Marechaussee Phantom!

**Potton:** What? Who let them in?

**Sigewinne:** Officer Morgane, before anything else, please find out Rawat's status.

**Potton:** Rawat...

**Morgane:** I don't see him... Hand over the hostage now, if you want any chance of avoiding an extension to your sentences.

**Potton:** Hahaha... Spare us your condescending attempts to coax us back to our cells! You think we're looking to negotiate? Rawat is dead!

**Sigewinne:** Potton...

**Morgane:** Arrest them all! Don't let anyone get away!

#### Talk to Potton

**Morgane:** Gotta say, I never expected to see the head nurse in combat...

**Sigewinne:** Some patients respond better to a more aggressive treatment plan. Did we get everyone?

**Dupond:** Captain, we've finished the headcount — Potton's missing...

**Paimon:** Over there! The door's closing!

**Potton:** Hah, one group trying to arrest me, another trying to hunt me down... Good, I get to kill two birds with one stone.

**Morgane:** How did he get up there? There must be a secret passage somewhere. Find it!

**Potton:** Don't waste your energy. I already sealed it off.

**Morgane:** How naive. We have more people outside. If we're not back out by the agreed time, they'll come to our rescue.

**Potton:** Well then, you'd better hope they get here in time!

**Sigewinne:** There's some kind of gas leaking from the pipes... Be careful, it's poisonous.

**Potton:** This is our distribution center, where we store the product derived from our source solution. We had to use special fire prevention methods to prevent contamination.

**Potton:** The pipe network here was designed to emit a gas for firefighting. One little modification later, and it makes an efficient death trap.

**Sigewinne:** Why would you do this? Your partners are here too.

**Potton:** Oh, please. If they can't supply what I need, they're of no further use to me. Besides, I can't have them exposing my potential whereabouts.

**Paimon:** He's a maniac... C'mon, we've gotta find a way to open that door!

**Potton:** Go ahead, struggle in vain. Once the last of you croaks, no one will ever find me again. So long!

**Sigewinne:** Are you sure you're ready to leave, Potton? You haven't changed your dressing today.

**Potton:** ...You can't cure my face. Just give up.

**Sigewinne:** And what if I could?

**Potton:** Hmph... Nice try, you're just trying to stall me. Don't you worry about me, I'll find a way to cure my face once I get out of here.

**Sigewinne:** Oh, I doubt it. After all, Fontainians can no longer dissolve in Primordial Seawater.

**Potton:** What the... How did you...!?

**Sigewinne:** The key component of the face-changing potion is the fluid left behind by dissolved Fontainians. It might look like ordinary water, but it has some unique elements in it.

**Sigewinne:** Before the flood, all Fontainians were Oceanids, physiologically speaking.

**Sigewinne:** A potion made from that fluid could dissolve and reconstruct the face of a Fontainian, in much the same way as Hydro Eidolons change form.

**Sigewinne:** Theoretically, it could give someone any appearance they wished.

**Sigewinne:** But since the flood, Fontainians have become true humans. They can no longer provide the source material for this potion, nor reap its benefits.

**Morgane:** Are you saying... this potion is made from Fontainians dissolved with Primordial Seawater? Then their crimes are far worse than I thought...

**Potton:** How could you know all of this? No, wait... Hahaha, of course! Well, well. So that's why you have a human appearance.

**Sigewinne:** It also means that I have a formula you're unaware of. *cough* ...The air's getting thicker... It's getting hard to breathe.

**Potton:** Heh, I might get to make a deal today after all. Alright, keep talking. How do you intend to give me the formula?

**Sigewinne:** Oh, it's not a formula, but a finished product. I have a bottle of it right here.

**Potton:** What?

**Sigewinne:** When I tried Rawat's skincare product, I noticed it had a certain special component in it. So I had my friends gather some other ingredients for me, and sure enough, a little experimenting later, I'd produced the potion.

**Sigewinne:** I'm guessing his skincare lotion contains the fruits of your joint research?

**Potton:** Huh. Impressive guesswork. Yeah, we were his supplier for some of the raw ingredients.

**Paimon:** *gasp*

**Potton:** But it only contains a tiny amount of the active ingredient. The effect is miniscule compared to the undiluted fluid.

**Sigewinne:** Well, I found a way to reverse the dilution process, giving the skincare lotion a similar effect to the fluid it is derived from.

**Potton:** That's impossible...

**Sigewinne:** If I tell you some of the ingredients, I think you can figure it out. First, you need to combine Tainted Hydro Phantasm tissue with Transoceanic Pearls at a ratio of around three to seven.

**Potton:** Tainted Hydro Phantasms and Transoceanic Pearls? Ah, I see... Can't believe I never thought of that...

**Sigewinne:** So. Can we make a deal?

**Potton:** Sounds good to me. There's a delivery pipeline beneath this window. Drop the potion in, and I'll release you all.

**Sigewinne:** Nope, not trusting you that easily. You're like those naughty kids who try to hide their illness. Open the sluice gate first, then I'll hand you the potion.

**Potton:** Hmph. Even if you're not bluffing, your potion won't cure my face permanently... You really think you have any leverage here?

**Potton:** I'm not closing the valve this time. Clock's ticking.

**Sigewinne:** Fine, then I'll just stand here with the potion in my hand. And when I faint from the poison, I'll drop it and it'll be destroyed.

**Potton:** No...

**Paimon:** It's open!

**Morgane:** Disable that sluice gate! Jam it! Don't let it close again!

**Sigewinne:** That's more like it. In that case, a promise is a promise.

**Morgane:** Head Nurse, there's too much poison gas in here. We have to leave, now!

#### Get out via the way you came

**Paimon:** Phew... That was too close! That Potton is a real nasty piece of work...

**Sigewinne:** Don't be too quick to sit down. Moving around a little will help relieve the soreness.

**Sigewinne:** Same goes for you, Traveler. But Paimon, you should be fine, right?

**Paimon:** Flying fast is pretty tiring too, actually! Anyway, that's not important right now. Paimon's still shocked to learn that there was a whole criminal operation going on behind that skincare lotion.

**Sigewinne:** Shame I ended up giving the improved version to Potton after finally making it for you... I'll make you some more, I promise.

**Paimon:** No no no, please don't... Paimon doesn't wanna use that stuff anymore. Still, it was a pretty good job that Paimon wanted to buy some in the first place, huh? It ended up saving everyone's lives! That's gotta count for something.

Sigewinne was the one doing the saving.

**Paimon:** Yeah, yeah, Paimon was just kidding...

**Morgane:** Head Nurse, I've done a final headcount of our team and the criminals. Everyone's here and doing fine. We're ready to escort the fugitives back to the Fortress of Meropide.

**Morgane:** It's a shame that we didn't find Rawat. At this point, I'm afraid he's probably deceased.

**Paimon:** *sigh* What a horrible bunch. Especially that Potton guy. If he hadn't made good on that deal, we'd all be dead because of him.

**Sigewinne:** No we wouldn't. I always keep a whole of bunch antidotes on me for emergencies, and one of them neutralizes that poison gas. We'd have been fine, even if he hadn't opened the gate.

**Paimon:** What? Then why did you give him the potion?

**Morgane:** ...If this is true, I must concur. Sorry to be blunt, but I think you made a bad decision by handing him that bottle.

**Morgane:** If he changes his appearance and flees Fontaine, it's unlikely that we'll ever find him, no matter how cooperative his accomplices are when we question them.

**Sigewinne:** No, you don't understand. I gave him the potion precisely so we could be sure where he'll go next.

**Morgane:** What do you mean?

**Sigewinne:** We gotta hurry. She won't last much longer.

#### Head to Potton's destination

**Ceasth:** Don't move a muscle! I might be short, but I pack a mean punch!

**Ceasth:** Captain? You're just in time. While I was lying in wait, I spotted this crook snooping around, trying to find a way in. So I detained him.

**Potton:** Hmph...

**Paimon:** Whoa, his face...

**Paimon:** What?

**Paimon:** Wait, so what's going on? Wasn't the whole point of the potion to disguise himself and escape Fontaine? Why would change back to his original face and run right into our trap!?

**Potton:** Enough of this nonsense! Let me in!

**Ceasth:** You better pipe down! There's an old lady in there who's very sick. No way are we letting a crook like you inside!

**Paimon:** He's gotta be lying, right? Rawat was a victim in all this, he was forced into it against his will... How could he and this monster be the same person?

**Potton:** Forced against his will? Hahahaha... That's the funniest thing I've heard all day.

**Potton:** Back when I was being coerced, everyone thought I was colluding of my own free will. And then when I actually became a criminal in my own right, you all suddenly thought I must be under duress!

**Potton:** Amazing what a difference a handsome face makes!

**Morgane:** Save us the speech. Explain yourself!

**Potton:** You want an explanation? Fine, I'll tell it how it is.

**Potton:** When I was just Potton, the whole world treated me with contempt.

**Audree:** Ew. What do you want? Get away from me...

**Potton:** Women despised me.

**Antibes:** Collaborate? Pff, you don't look like much of a researcher to me...

**Potton:** My peers scorned me.

**"Enri":** Mr. Potton, was it? I'm most interested in your research. Consider my offer carefully — I doubt you'll find other investment opportunities.

**Potton:** Only villains would work with me.

**Houilles:** You want out of the game? Forget about it. Turns out I slipped up a little — left your picture with some Marechaussee Phantom officers... Easy mistake to make. Now they've listed you as an accomplice and drawn up an arrest warrant.

**Houilles:** So I suggest you put any thoughts of running away to rest.... If you get caught by the Gardes for being in the Sinthe business, you ain't ever seeing the light of day again! Hahahaha...

**Potton:** There was no way out for me...

**Potton:** Until... I could stop being Potton.

**Audree:** Mr. Rawat, I, um... I have something I'd like to tell you.

**Rawat:** I became popular with women.

**Antibes:** This new potion you've developed is fascinating — genius, in fact! Would you consider working with me? I'd be happy to sign a licensing agreement...

**Rawat:** My peers looked up to me.

**"Enri":** I'm very interested in your research, Sir. You must no doubt have offers from countless investors, so far be it from me to ask for a full collaboration, per se — I merely wanted to express my interest... No pressure at all, naturally.

**Rawat:** And the villains even began to fear me.

**Rawat:** Becoming Rawat showed me what a superficial world we live in.

**Rawat:** But I did not hold a grudge. After all, this superficiality could be made to work in my favor.

**Rawat:** Or so I thought... But alas, the world seemed bent on finding a reason to make me despise it.

**Rawat:** Mother, I'm back! How are you doing?

**Sick Old Lady:** ...Hmm... Who are you?

**Rawat:** I bought the best medicine Mora could buy, but nothing would cure her illness... In the end, I hoped at least that she could see her son one last time before she passed, but...

**Rawat:** ...When everyone else rejected me, the only person who cared about me was my mother. Then, when I became a rising star in the Fontaine Research Institute, I was unrecognizable to her.

**Potton:** I have to let her see me again, even if it means donning my own wretched, ugly face...

**Potton:** ...Well? Is that enough explaining for you? Now do you understand? Let me inside already!

**Morgane:** Have you ever considered if you'll even be able to look your mother in the eyes, after what you've done?

**Morgane:** You found a way to completely change your identity. You could easily have escaped from them... But no, you chose to keep working with them!

**Potton:** Hmph, why would I want to give up on such a lucrative business? The profits were dozens of times that of the Sinthe business, and it was all above board!

**Paimon:** So you kept being a villain just for the money!

**Potton:** Villain? By what standard? Is doing evil deeds really what makes someone a villain in your eyes, or does it all come down to the way they look? None of you saw a villain when I was wearing my other face, did you?

**Potton:** You have no right to judge me! You're all just fake people living in a hypocritical world. I regret nothing! My mother would understand!

**Paimon:** You... You're a villain to the core! But especially that tongue!

**Sigewinne:** Honestly, I don't see a villain when I look at your current face, either.

**Potton:** Don't humor me. You don't seem surprised by my confession in the least. How long have you known the truth?

**Sigewinne:** I didn't know anything for sure until you chose to take that potion bottle and spare our lives. But if you're asking when I started suspecting you, it was the first time you came to see me at the infirmary.

**Sigewinne:** I had to confirm my suspicions. That's why I chose to join this investigation.

**Potton:** Are you kidding me? A young, promising researcher and a wretched criminal? What on earth gave you the idea that they could be one and the same person?

**Sigewinne:** Well, there were no visual clues — this potion's face-changing effects are very powerful. Even as a Melusine, I couldn't see through the disguise.

**Sigewinne:** But I believe I understand humanity a little better than most of my kind. Humans have certain fundamental qualities that do not change with their appearance.

**Potton:** Fundamental qualities, huh? So, you think you're the one who's seen through me, do you?

**Potton:** Most people probably don't know this, but I think I can guess what your deal is. Melusine constitutions are very close to that of water, so it stands to reason that the potion should work on them, too.

**Potton:** You used it yourself, didn't you? That's why you have a human appearance.

**Paimon:** Sigewinne?

**Sigewinne:** Yes, you're right.

**Potton:** Haha... I'll bet it was back in the days when Melusines were discriminated against because of their appearance.

**Potton:** It was either carry on living in the gutters as part of an alien race cast out by humanity, or become the Head Nurse of the Fortress of Meropide, revered by all. Not a hard choice to make. It's a no-brainer.

**Potton:** So you see? You and I, we're the same! And I'm the one who saw through you!

**Sigewinne:** That's not important right now. You came here to see your mother, didn't you? Well, I can grant you your wish.

**Potton:** Huh...?

**Morgane:** Head Nurse, I'm not sure that's a good idea in his current state.

**Sigewinne:** Don't worry. Back in the warehouse when I traded the potion for our lives, I was doing it to test him.

**Sigewinne:** He was willing to risk sacrificing his only chance of escaping capture by opening the gate in exchange for the potion, all so he could see his mother again. If nothing else, his concern for his mother is genuine.

**Morgane:** I see...

**Sigewinne:** So before you arrest him, please allow him to see his mother one last time. Traveler and I will accompany him — you have nothing to worry about.

**Morgane:** Alright. I understand.

#### Enter Potton's mother's residence

**Potton:** Mom? It's me, Potton, your son! You recognize me now... don't you?

**Sick Old Lady:** *cough*

**Potton:** Mom...! What's wrong?

**Sigewinne:** I'm sorry, but I have some bad news. I examined your mother last time we were here, and it appears that due to her prolonged fever, she's... lost her eyesight.

**Potton:** What?

**Sigewinne:** Your appearance no longer has any bearing on her ability to recognize you. But... there are always other ways to remind our loved ones who we are... wouldn't you say?

**Potton:** No no, it can't be... Please, Mom, open your eyes! It's me, your son, Potton! If... If you really can't see my face, then... here, take my hand... Tell me you feel your son's hand!

**Sick Old Lady:** Hmm...

**Potton:** I've done so much to get to this point... Please, you have to recognize me...

**Sick Old Lady:** *cough*

**Potton:** No, no, no... No!

**Sigewinne:** Hm, the temperature of his palm must have changed. ...I'm sorry. It seems I couldn't bring him back to you after all.

**Potton:** Mom... Why will you answer her, and not me? Why? Why!?

### The Truth of What Was

The many-layered mysterious case caused by cosmetics has now been fully solved, and after Sigewinne finishes helping the Marechaussee Phantom wind up the case, she prepares to tell you a story about the past...

#### Take a look around the Court of Fontaine

**Calcagni:** The wait is over, everyone! The sale of our new skincare product, Romaritime Essence, will now begin!

**Paimon:** Another skincare promotion? And it's selling as fast as last time! Wonder what people would think if they knew what was in that last batch...

**Paimon:** Oh, Sigewinne's here too!

**Paimon:** Hey, Sigewinne!

**Sigewinne:** Oh, Traveler, Paimon, hello! I hope you two got a good rest yesterday after all your hard work?

**Paimon:** Of course! Nurse's orders, right? ...Anyway, are you here to take notes for your next article?

**Sigewinne:** Actually, I'm mostly here to help Officer Morgane wrap up yesterday's case.

**Paimon:** Ah, that reminds Paimon... That stuff sold like crazy, didn't it! Is it gonna cause huge problems?

**Sigewinne:** Nope, there's nothing to worry about. Unless modified, Butterfly Dew is an excellent skincare product that's completely safe to use.

**Sigewinne:** Besides, officers have already retrieved all Butterfly Dew purchased on the market. Now, it's just a matter of disposal.

**Sigewinne:** After consulting Monsieur Neuvillette, I came up with a way to neutralize any harmful effects, so it'll evaporate naturally without polluting the water or soil.

**Paimon:** Wow! It's Sigewinne to the rescue again!

**Morgane:** Thank you for serving as our consultant, Head Nurse. All the Butterfly Dew we recovered has been disposed of according to your instructions.

**Sigewinne:** Great. Good job, everyone.

**Morgane:** Also, you mentioned you wanted a copy of Rawat's interrogation records... Well, technically Potton's records... Anyway, I have them with me. Here.

**Morgane:** After he saw his mother for the last time, it was like all his mental defenses came crashing down. He put up no resistance during the interrogation, and answered all our questions. We learned a lot.

**Morgane:** Apparently, the formula was something he discovered by chance while helping the criminal gang produce Sinthe. Later, he came up with the idea to dilute it and use the resulting substance as a skincare product.

**Morgane:** Their supplier was Vacher — but you could also argue that they were the ones doing him a favor. His operation helped Vacher's gang dispose of an enormous amount of evidence.

**Morgane:** It made for a seamless collaboration. One man's trash was another man's treasure, and there were no loose ends. It's no wonder we never noticed what Potton's gang was up to.

**Paimon:** Vacher again, huh... Is there still no end to the harm he caused?

**Morgane:** Make no mistake, the crimes of Vacher and his accomplices will come to an end. It's only a matter of time.

#### Go to the place you agreed upon with Sigewinne

**Paimon:** Oh... Neuvillette and Wriothesley! They're here too!

**Paimon:** So the old friend she mentioned was her teacher then? The "witch"?

**Paimon:** Uh, sorry, sorry, Paimon didn't mean to be rude! It's only 'cause Sigewinne calls her that all the time, so...

**Wriothesley:** I doubt she considers that title to be offensive, Paimon. Don't worry.

**Neuvillette:** Indeed. In fact, I believe Sigewinne would take it as a sign of affection. She has spoken of this doctor on many occasions, and it sounds like she was a truly generous individual.

**Neuvillette:** In those days, many people harbored prejudices against Melusines. She was the only teacher willing to take Sigewinne on as a student.

**Paimon:** Wow, it's really nice of you two to be here for Sigewinne! Guess you guys really look out for your younger colleagues, huh...

**Wriothesley:** Younger colleague? That might be true for the Iudex, but I'm not sure I can say the same.

**Wriothesley:** Sigewinne served a sentence in the Fortress of Meropide hundreds of years ago, and after she did her time, she decided to stick around. She's been an integral part of our administration ever since.

**Wriothesley:** Strictly speaking, the head nurse has been around longer than most people in the Fortress, myself included. Pretty sure that makes me the younger colleague.

**Paimon:** She served a sentence?

She committed a crime?

**Neuvillette:** Yes. Long before I became Iudex, there was an ancient law in Fontaine that prohibited any attempt to transform another species into a human.

**Neuvillette:** Initially, I and most researchers believed this law to have been imposed due to ethical concerns.

**Neuvillette:** But now, it seems more likely that the law was nothing more than an insurance policy — a way for Egeria to ensure that her people would remain insulated from the truth, thus enabling them to lead more straightforward, happier lives.

**Wriothesley:** Ignorance is bliss, as they say.

**Neuvillette:** Sigewinne had her reasons for choosing to obtain a human form, but the act was nonetheless in clear violation of the law.

**Paimon:** Could you tell us a bit more about what happened back then?

**Neuvillette:** Certainly. Sigewinne does not object to her close friends learning about her history. In fact, I first learned of it myself because she confessed the truth to me of her own volition...

**"The Witch":** Even with their daughter so ill, they kicked you out? Stubborn as always... and prejudice isn't a disease I can cure, either.

**"The Witch":** *sigh* Shame that I don't have the energy to get over there. You're laughable, you old witch. You want to save everyone in Fontaine, but you can't even save the patient before you.

**Sigewinne:** Master, what are you...

**"The Witch":** I discovered a strange disease, one I suspect that every Fontainian suffers from. But without witnesses or proof, no one will believe me.

**"The Witch":** I had no choice but to experiment on myself to find a cure, but I hit a wall — and as you can see, it took a wretched toll on me.

**Sigewinne:** Then... I'll find a cure for you, and then you can go cure her!

**"The Witch":** There's no need... I'm beyond saving now. There's nothing you can do.

**Sigewinne:** But Master...

**"The Witch":** As I said before, a doctor's duty is to treat whatever ailments they can.

**Sigewinne:** I know... But, they don't trust me at all.

**"The Witch":** *cough* That girl... She's your first ever patient, isn't she? ...Tell me, what cost are you willing to bear to see her cured?

**Sigewinne:** I'll do whatever it takes.

**"The Witch":** Even if it means breaking the law and being punished for it?

**Sigewinne:** ...As long as I don't hurt anyone, yes.

**"The Witch":** Haha. Spoken like a true student of mine.

**"The Witch":** Then I shall make one more final gamble... and see my experiment through to its end. Leave me for now, and come back in two hours.

**"The Witch":** If I am able to work this miracle, then I will have found a way to save all Fontainians, and I shall be able to help you treat that girl.

**"The Witch":** If not... Then it means there is one more disease in this world that I cannot treat. In that case, do not come looking for me.

**"The Witch":** I will leave you a parting gift — an accidental discovery, happened upon through my study of this strange disease. It has little use, but it will at least help you cure this one patient you can reach.

**Neuvillette:** When Sigewinne returned, the miracle had not occurred.

**Neuvillette:** She found a potion bottle and a note on the table. After using the potion as instructed, she gained her current form, breaking Fontaine's law in the process.

**Neuvillette:** And after I had confirmed the veracity of the events, I gave my verdict.

**Paimon:** So that's what happened...

**Neuvillette:** After reading the Marechaussee Phantom's reports, I had a feeling a trip here might be in order.

#### Talk to Sigewinne

**Sigewinne:** You're here.

**Sigewinne:** The old friend I mentioned was my teacher. When she passed, her body was nowhere to be found. All that's left of her is an empty tomb, and even that's deep underwater now.

- **Traveler:** A truly odd doctor.

**Sigewinne:** Well, even if you chose to destroy it, I still thought I should give it to you first. You two did so much for me — how could I break my promise?

**Paimon:** Well, fair enough. But anyway, Paimon's definitely not interested in this stuff anymore.

Paimon...

**Paimon:** Uh, well...

**Paimon:** Heh, what was Paimon thinking? You made up your mind a long time ago. Yeah, let's just destroy this thing.

**Sigewinne:** Leave it to me.

**贝尔特朗:** 偶尔让我听到了，也都是一些跟药品制售有关的事，那家伙果然很重视研究成果的商业化。

#### 和逐影庭警员们会合

**派蒙:** 真是意外，我原本以为拉瓦特是一个热情、开朗的帅哥研究员，没想到其他研究员眼里的他跟我的第一印象差别这么大。

**希格雯:** 那派蒙现在对他的印象是什么样的？

**派蒙:** 嗯…因为家境不好，所以孤高，逐利的帅哥研究员？

**希格雯:** 对外貌的印象没有改变呢。

**派蒙:** 我承认我比较在意这一点啦，按理说那么帅气的一位研究员，就算家境不太好，也不至于如此孤僻吧？

**希格雯:** 不能仅仅通过外貌来判断一个人的全部哦。

**莫嘉娜:** 护士长还有旅行者，你们已经找那些研究员打听过了吧？有什么新的情报吗？

*双方将收集到的信息相互交流了一番。*

**希格雯:** 那位贝尔特朗提到了拉瓦特接触过制售药品的商贩，他注册过药品相关的品牌吗？

**希娅:** 拉瓦特是大案的检举人，所以我查阅过他的资料，并不记得他名下有药品相关的品牌。

**希格雯:** 很奇怪，他那么注重商业化，也反感向别人分享技术，要做药品生意的话，怎么会不注册自己的品牌呢？

**希格雯:** 会不会是不能让公众知道的合作？

**希娅:** 护士长难道是指和那些犯人合作？那些犯人的入狱罪名确实和药品制售有关。

**派蒙:** 不会吧？明明那些犯人就是拉瓦特主动举报的啊。

**希格雯:** 得了蛀牙的孩子来找我时，往往已经痛了很久，还要隐瞒自己吃过很多糖果。在这一点上，大人和小孩子没有太大差别哦。

**派蒙:** 话是这么说…

**莫嘉娜:** 处理举报信息的时候我见过他，我并不觉得他那样的人会跟那些犯人同流合污，更有可能是被胁迫的吧？

**莫嘉娜:** 就像护士长和旅行者提到的那样，他家境不太好。那么那些犯人就有可能打着挣钱的幌子找上他，让他提供学术上的支持。

**莫嘉娜:** 等到他发现那些犯人的真面目，恐怕已经陷入其中，被抓了把柄，不得不继续合作…这样的案例，我见过的只多不少。

**派蒙:** 这倒是很有道理，如果他一直遭受罪犯胁迫的话，那尽量避免与其他研究员合作也就情有可原了。

**派蒙:** 怪不得他那样的人会这么孤僻，被罪犯盯着，肯定每天都提心吊胆吧？

**希格雯:** 这样的话，好像更糟糕了。

**希格雯:** 如果拉瓦特和那些犯人一直有合作，他们肯定比我们更了解拉瓦特的行踪，很有可能赶在我们前面找到他。

**希娅:** 从刚刚在科学院里收集到的信息来看，拉瓦特最后一次出现在科学院是两天前，之后便一直没有人再见过他，会不会已经…

**派蒙:** 两天前？难道是在那次促销会结束之后？

**莫嘉娜:** 他也是在那天举报了犯人，这可很不妙。

**希格雯:** 嗯…大家先不要担心，我们应该还有机会。如果是举报那些犯人之后立刻失踪的话，更像是主动躲起来。

**派蒙:** 对啊，他肯定也害怕被报复吧。

**莫嘉娜:** 那我们现在必须尽快找到他，唯一的线索，应该就是之前提到的他母亲的家吧。希娅，这个能查出来吗？

**希娅:** 没问题，注册品牌时，枫丹廷会要求对方提供合规的住址信息，我记得拉瓦特填写的地址在枫丹廷郊外，那里应该就是。

**希格雯:** 那事不宜迟，我们出发吧！

#### 前往拉瓦特母亲的住处

**希娅:** 这里应该就是地址上的那间房屋了。

**派蒙:** 喂？有人吗？

**希格雯:** 请问您是拉瓦特的母亲吗？我们想打听一下他的下落。

**希格雯:** 旅行者和派蒙，这次又要麻烦你们了。

**希格雯:** 嗯，她已经在弥留之际了。

**派蒙:** 哦…

**派蒙:** 哦哦！

**派蒙:** 希格雯，你遇到这种情况一定很难受吧？

**希格雯:** 嗯…确实会有一些难受的感觉。

**希格雯:** 在美露莘看来，人类的生老病死就像是日升日落那样的自然规律。

**希格雯:** 然而人类的医学却可以在某种程度上违逆这种规律，简直是奇迹一样的科学，这也是我最初迷上医学的原因。

**希格雯:** 不过，等到我真的成为医生之后，才明白奇迹并不会总是发生，再高明的医生也有治不好的病。

### 地下的罪行

在拉瓦特母亲住处意外发现的线索，成为了让调查能够继续下去的关键情报。

**派蒙:** 是莫嘉娜警官，他们已经到了！

**波顿:** 那场大水过后，枫丹人的体质发生了很大变化，已经不能很好地适配这种易容药水了，副作用就是像我这样。

**波顿:** 再说了，你们每个人都私藏了一瓶备用药水的事，直到计划越狱之前不也是一直瞒着我？

**昂利:** 大伙都冷静一下，现在就是这么个情况，吵架也改变不了任何事情。

**昂利:** 波顿，你专门带我们到这里来，应该是有什么办法吧？

**昂利:** 这…

**波顿:** 拉瓦特…

#### 和波顿对话

**莫嘉娜:** 没想到护士长也有动武的时候…

**希格雯:** 那场大洪水之前，枫丹人本质上都是纯水精灵。

**希格雯:** 利用这种成分制成的药剂可以将枫丹人的外貌溶解、重构，从而达到水形幻灵那种程度的塑形效果。

### 曾经的真相

由一款美妆产品引发的重重谜案终于被彻底告破，希格雯协助逐影庭完成了最后的收尾工作，而她也准备好向你讲述一段往事…

#### 在枫丹廷四处逛逛

**卡嘉妮:** 让大家久等了，护肤新品「海露精华液」的发售正式开始！

**派蒙:** 又是护肤品的促销会吗？生意还和上次一样火爆呢，如果知道上次买的护肤品含有什么成分，真好奇他们会怎么想。

**派蒙:** 哦？希格雯今天也在这里。

**派蒙:** 你好啊，希格雯。

**希格雯:** 你们好啊，旅行者、派蒙。昨天忙碌了那么久，有好好休息吗？

**派蒙:** 当然了，你特别叮嘱过的。你今天又是来取材的吗？

**希格雯:** 主要是帮助莫嘉娜警官给昨天那件案子收尾。

**派蒙:** 我才想起来，那种东西卖出了那么多，会不会搞出什么大问题？

**希格雯:** 不用担心哦，没有被调制过的「蝶彩露」只是一款优质的护肤品，使用了也不会有事。

**希格雯:** 另外，警官们已经将售出的「蝶彩露」全部追回，正在安排集中销毁。

**希格雯:** 我请教了那维莱特先生，设计了一种将它无害化的方法，可以让它自然挥发掉而且不会污染水体和土壤。

**派蒙:** 希格雯果然还是很可靠啊。

**莫嘉娜:** 护士长，辛苦您在这边当顾问了，被追回的「蝶彩露」已经全部按照您给的方法销毁完毕。

**希格雯:** 嗯，也辛苦你们了。

**莫嘉娜:** 此外，您之前说想要一份拉瓦特的审讯记录…确切来说是波顿的审讯记录，我已经整理好了，现在交给您。

**莫嘉娜:** 昨天他见完母亲最后一面，心理防线就好像彻底崩溃了一样，对审讯完全没有抗拒，让我们问出了很多东西。

**莫嘉娜:** 他是在帮助犯罪团伙调制乐斯时意外发现了这种药水的配方，之后又想到了将它的稀释版本设计成护肤品。

**莫嘉娜:** 他们的供货方是那个瓦谢，或者应该说他们帮助瓦谢的团伙销毁了大量犯罪证据。

**莫嘉娜:** 这种合作简直天衣无缝，以至于我们之前一直没有注意到他们这个团伙。

**派蒙:** 那个瓦谢的影响到现在还没有完全消除吗？

**莫嘉娜:** 瓦谢一伙遗留的罪恶注定要被全部消灭，只是时间早晚的问题。

**莫嘉娜:** 那场大水过后他们失去了货源，被逼得铤而走险，试图用私藏的胎海水主动袭击落单的路人，当然最后都以失败收场。

**莫嘉娜:** 频繁接到报案后我们盯上了这个团伙，并最终把他们揪了出来。

**派蒙:** 嗯，这样才对嘛。

**莫嘉娜:** 按他供述的说法，我们的人已经快要锁定他了，即便他作为拉瓦特的时候很注重和那些犯人保持距离。

**莫嘉娜:** 他主动举报，其实是想用检举人的身份暂时摆脱嫌疑，争取一些时间，好让他能易容回波顿的样子，去见母亲最后一面。

**莫嘉娜:** 但是他没料到，即便「波顿」这个人已经消失了很久，我们的人依旧盯得很紧，他易容后刚出现在公众视野里就被抓了。

**派蒙:** 他之前就尝试过一次？看来他真的很想让母亲见自己一面啊，最后却变成这样…

**希格雯:** 从审讯记录附带的罪证来看，他易容成拉瓦特后赚的第一笔钱，全都用来给他母亲治病了？

**莫嘉娜:** 是的，所以我怀疑过，他最初决定使用那种药水，是否并非出自贪念，而是仅仅想利用另一个身份来救治自己的亲人呢？

**莫嘉娜:** 但他终究还是走得太远了，最后，只能落得个罪有应得的下场。
